HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001525/B011"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_03d56254cd0a9a2b25e0","text":"102:7 most changed this ayah: \"عين اليقين\" made زرتم المقابر read as an interim visit before a direct encounter with the thing itself, not as final arrival. 102:8 added another surprise through ن ع م B011/B012, pleasant settlement and walking on foot; the grave-visit is retrospectively one leg in a whole path of dwelling, movement, and eventual questioning. The hiddenness of قبر B002 became stronger after the later seeing-language, because concealment now looks like the stage before exposure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000654/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_18e29e1a6df9879517c2","text":"2. **The end-point of a bent course.** Fixed `ز و ر B001` contributes turning aside or deviation as a dormant coloring of the visit-form. Structurally, contextual `ل ه و B001` has already described being turned from one concern by another, and `ك ث ر B002` names the rivalry that drives the course until the graves. The branch's assigned role is spatial: distraction becomes a path bent away from its proper bearing. The packet does not state that rivalry causes deviation; I supply that directional arrow from occupation to misorientation. `زُرْتُمُ` then registers more than arrival: the grave-visit exposes where a repeatedly diverted trajectory has actually led.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000654/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2372e1454706224c094f","text":"1. **A temporary act at a place of apparent settlement.** Fixed `زُرْتُمُ` anchors `ز و ر B003` (visiting and intentionally turning toward someone), while fixed `ٱلْمَقَابِرَ` anchors `ق ب ر B001` (the dead person's grave and resting place). The conjunction `حَتَّىٰ` makes this encounter the limit of the preceding rivalry, yet 102:3–7 continue from graves into knowledge and direct sight. Visit supplies temporariness; grave supplies enclosure and apparent repose. I infer from the continuation that the visitors do not reach their final endpoint there. The ayah consequently recasts the cemetery from terminal destination into a threshold on a longer itinerary.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000654/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_412c864100e34ad603df","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:2:2\",\"root_000654\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Mezara varış, çokluk yarışının saptırdığı yaşam yönünün nereye çıktığını mekânsal olarak gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001195/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_42466571aa8a5f6a3cf9","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:2:3\",\"root_001195\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Defin görünürlüğü geçici olarak keser; sonraki bilgi ve görme, gizlenenin silinmediğini, açığa çıkmayı beklediğini gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000654/B003","root_001195/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_63f8fc60775d4ce9ef49","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:2:2\",\"root_000654\",[\"B003\"]],[\"102:2:3\",\"root_001195\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Mezar, sahip olunan son durak değil, sonradan gelecek açığa çıkışa ve sorguya geçilen geçici bir eşik olur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000654/B003","root_001195/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8056bd773936cf7fd084","text":"1. **The grave as a visited station, not possessed endpoint.** Branch evidence: ز و ر B003, visiting and turning toward someone; ق ب ر B001, grave/cemetery; س ء ل B001, questioning; ر ء ي B001, seeing. Structural cue: \"حتى\" gives a limit to the distraction, while \"زرتم\" uses visiting language for the cemetery. Assigned role: ز و ر B003 makes the grave a place of temporary arrival; سأل/رأي make what follows an onward exposure. Abductive move: I infer from later questioning and seeing that the visit does not close the sequence. Reading change: the ayah reads as a threshold-visit into disclosure, not a final stop.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000654/B001","root_000654/B002","root_001195/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_835966ef545ce4b762a2","text":"2. **Deviation reaches the graves.** Branch evidence: ز و ر B001, turning aside/deviation; ز و ر B002, falsehood and invalid speech; ل ه و B001, diversion; ك ث ر B002, rivalry; ق ب ر B001, graves. Structural cue: the root of \"visit\" also carries deviation and falsehood branches, and the previous ayah has a diversion caused by rivalry. Assigned role: ز و ر B001/B002 color the \"visit\" as a swerving course produced by false evaluation. Abductive move: I infer that the same movement toward a place can disclose the crookedness of the path that led there. Abductive move: I supply the temporal arrow: what goes inward at قبر becomes legible through later knowledge and sight. Reading change: the cemetery becomes a concealment-device that paradoxically prepares revelation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001195/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e05f033ff4fa3b876414","text":"3. **A concealment chamber that cannot prevent manifestation.** Fixed `ق ب ر B002` gathers hiddenness, sinking, hollowness, and enclosure. Later in the clipped window, `ع ل م B001` repeats as disclosure, `ي ق ن B001` removes doubt, and 102:6–7 move to `ر ء ي B001` and contextual `ع ي ن B002` (face-to-face seeing). The graves' assigned role is the maximum spatial concealment between distraction and manifestation. I infer that what is sunk from present sight remains available for later disclosure; the packet gives the sequence but not that causal rule. `ٱلْمَقَابِرَ` therefore reads as delayed visibility rather than erasure: burial interrupts ordinary sight but is structurally overcome by knowing and eyewitness certainty.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000654/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e176491aea7ddac39474","text":"4. **Exploratory: entry into the place where competitive appearances fail.** Fixed `ز و ر B002` supplies falsehood or validity bent away from truth, and fixed `ق ب ر B002` supplies hiddenness. These dormant branches are activated by the movement from status-rivalry (`ك ث ر B002`) to repeated knowing and seeing. False appearance is assigned to the public ranking produced by rivalry; hiddenness is assigned to the grave that removes those displays. I infer, rather than read explicitly, that competitive status depends on crafted appearances and that burial strips them away.
